If run has no descendants with localname 't' and no other meaningful children, remove\n            has_t = any(localname(d.tag) == 't' and (d.text or '') != '' for d in child.iter())\n            # Keep run if it has non-t children (e.g., controls) even if text empty\n            has_non_t = any(localname(d.tag) not in ('run', 't') for d in child.iter())\n            if (not has_t) and (not has_non_t):\n                p.remove(child)\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache(elem):\n    # Remove any <hp:lineSegArray> / <hp:linesegarray> nodes under elem\n    for child in list(elem):\n        ln = localname(child.tag)\n        if ln.lower() in ('linesegarray', 'linesegarray'):  # same, but keep intent explicit\n            elem.remove(child)\n        elif ln in ('lineSegArray', 'linesegarray'):\n            elem.remove(child)\n        else:\n            remove_layout_cache(child)\n\n\ndef update_fields_in_tree(tree: ET.ElementTree, replacements: list[tuple[str, str]]):\n    root = tree.getroot()\n    modified_paras = set()\n\n    #
